.. _api-library-c-abi:

C ABI
=====

The C function tables for GUI.Forms and GUI.Drawing and the C++ convenience wrapper over them: the sealed ABI 1 (gui_forms/c/) and the experimental drawing tables.

Types
-----

.. list-table::
   :class: autosummary longtable
   :widths: 35 65

   * - :cpp:class:`ApplicationTable <gui_forms::abi::ApplicationTable>`
     - gf_application_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_APPLICATION.
   * - :cpp:class:`AutomationTable <gui_forms::abi::AutomationTable>`
     - gf_automation_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_AUTOMATION.
   * - :cpp:class:`BindingsTable <gui_forms::abi::BindingsTable>`
     - gf_bindings_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_BINDINGS.
   * - :cpp:class:`ClassesTable <gui_forms::abi::ClassesTable>`
     - gf_classes_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_CLASSES.
   * - :cpp:class:`ContentTable <gui_forms::abi::ContentTable>`
     - gf_content_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_CONTENT.
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlsTable <gui_forms::abi::ControlsTable>`
     - gf_controls_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_CONTROLS.
   * - :cpp:class:`CustomControlsTable <gui_forms::abi::CustomControlsTable>`
     - gf_custom_controls_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_CUSTOM_CONTROLS.
   * - :cpp:class:`DataGridTable <gui_forms::abi::DataGridTable>`
     - gf_data_grid_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_DATA_GRID.
   * - :cpp:class:`DiagnosticsTable <gui_forms::abi::DiagnosticsTable>`
     - gf_diagnostics_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_DIAGNOSTICS.
   * - :cpp:class:`DispatchTable <gui_forms::abi::DispatchTable>`
     - gf_dispatch_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_DISPATCH.
   * - :cpp:class:`DockingTable <gui_forms::abi::DockingTable>`
     - gf_docking_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_DOCKING.
   * - :cpp:class:`EventsTable <gui_forms::abi::EventsTable>`
     - gf_events_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_EVENTS.
   * - :cpp:class:`FontsTable <gui_forms::abi::FontsTable>`
     - gf_fonts_table, negotiated as GF_TABLE_FONTS.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_api_v0 <gd_api_v0>`
     - gd_api_v0 is the size-versioned negotiated function table for drawing object ownership, transforms, paths, regions, brushes, pens, fonts, text, images, bitmap locks/edits/damage, recording/replay, native interop, and raster publication.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_bitmap_damage_summary <gd_bitmap_damage_summary>`
     - gd_bitmap_damage_summary projects revision, rectangle/compaction/collapse counts, and exact bounds after bitmap edits.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_bitmap_edit_view <gd_bitmap_edit_view>`
     - gd_bitmap_edit_view exposes bounded mutable premultiplied BGRA storage, dimensions, stride, and generational edit token.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_bitmap_lock_view <gd_bitmap_lock_view>`
     - gd_bitmap_lock_view exposes bounded pixel pointer, dimensions, signed stride, pixel format, and lease token for one active read/write lock.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_color <gd_color>`
     - gd_color projects packed straight-alpha ARGB plus explicit empty-color identity.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_color_remap <gd_color_remap>`
     - gd_color_remap maps one exact source ARGB value to a replacement for image-attribute processing.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_corner_radii <gd_corner_radii>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_error_view <gd_error_view>`
     - gd_error_view returns thread-local drawing result code and borrowed bounded diagnostic.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_font_resolution <gd_font_resolution>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_grid_pattern <gd_grid_pattern>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_handle <gd_handle>`
     - gd_handle encodes one-based drawing registry slot plus generation for deterministic stale-object rejection.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_marker_style <gd_marker_style>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_matrix <gd_matrix>`
     - gd_matrix projects the six coefficients of a 2D affine transform.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_point <gd_point>`
     - gd_point projects one floating-point drawing coordinate.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_rect <gd_rect>`
     - gd_rect projects floating-point drawing rectangle geometry.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_rect_i <gd_rect_i>`
     - gd_rect_i projects exact integer pixel bounds for bitmap lock/edit/damage contracts.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_size <gd_size>`
     - gd_size projects floating-point drawing dimensions.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_string_view <gd_string_view>`
     - gd_string_view is a non-owning bounded drawing-ABI string view with explicit validation before copying.
   * - :cpp:class:`gd_text_item <gd_text_item>`

.. cpp:struct:: gf_application_config

   ``#include <gui_forms/c/gf_abi.h>`` (:src:`source <include/gui_forms/c/gf_abi.h>`)

   gf_application_config (C ABI 1.1) is what application.create makes the library's application with: the theme base, up to 16 packages compiled after it, and the presentation source. With GF_PRESENTATION_SOURCE_HOST windows start from GUI.Forms' defaults and follow the host's presentation reports (a later minor may start from the host's settings read at create); with GF_PRESENTATION_SOURCE_DEFAULTS they start from explicit defaults and still follow the host's reports.

   .. cpp:member:: uint32_t struct_size

   .. cpp:member:: gf_theme_base theme_base

   .. cpp:member:: const gf_theme_package* packages

   .. cpp:member:: uint64_t package_count

   .. cpp:member:: gf_presentation_source presentation_source

   .. cpp:member:: uint32_t reserved

.. cpp:namespace:: 0

.. cpp:struct:: gf_application_table

   ``#include <gui_forms/c/gf_abi.h>`` (:src:`source <include/gui_forms/c/gf_abi.h>`)

   ic: gf_abi::application: generated from include/gui_forms/c/gf_abi_application.ic.toml by tools/ic_declarations.py; ic: edit the declaration, not these lines. Thread: ui, unless a call says otherwise.


   .. rubric:: Methods

   .. cpp:member:: gf_status (*create)(const gf_application_config* config, gf_theme_report* report)

      ic: Fails invalid_argument (GF_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT for an unknown base or source, a non-zero reserved field, ic: or packages null with a non-zero count.), unchanged; invalid_state (GF_STATUS_INVALID_STATE, making nothing ic: and leaving the findings, while an application is current or while the library holds objects made without ic: one;), unchanged.

   .. cpp:member:: gf_status (*close)(uint64_t* windows_alive)

      ic: Requires not_null(windows_alive), else returns GF_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT, unchanged. Fails invalid_state ic: (GF_STATUS_INVALID_STATE, changing nothing, while any is; once closed, the theme is the built-in one again ic: (as themes.reset), windows made afterwards use the process default and create may make another application.), ic: unchanged.

   .. cpp:member:: gf_status (*state)(gf_application_state* state)

      ic: Requires not_null(state), else returns GF_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT, unchanged.

   .. cpp:member:: gf_status (*set_program_identity)(const gf_program_identity* identity)

      ic: Fails invalid_argument (GF_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT for a null or short record, a non-zero reserved field, ic: text that is not UTF-8 or a website that is neither empty nor an https:// address.), unchanged.

   .. cpp:member:: uint32_t struct_size

   .. cpp:member:: uint32_t abi_minor

.. cpp:struct:: gf_runtime_table

   ``#include <gui_forms/c/gf_abi.h>`` (:src:`source <include/gui_forms/c/gf_abi.h>`)

   gf_runtime_table (GF_TABLE_RUNTIME) reports the versions and copies out or clears the calling thread's error detail, from any thread. Since 1.1 it also shows the splashscreen before any object or window (splashscreen_show, set_status, set_progress, close), on the thread that will be the UI thread; the library's first window hands it over.

.. cpp:struct:: gf_splashscreen_options

   ``#include <gui_forms/c/gf_abi.h>`` (:src:`source <include/gui_forms/c/gf_abi.h>`)

   gf_splashscreen_options (C ABI 1.1) is what runtime.splashscreen_show shows: PNG artwork whose pixel size is the window's (160 to 1600 per extent), a UTF-8 status line and an optional progress in [0, 1], in fixed built-in styling (owner ruling Q-S1 (a)).

      ic: Admitted in created; then ended. In running: returns GF_STATUS_INVALID_STATE, unchanged. In ended: returns ic: GF_STATUS_DISPOSED, unchanged. Fails invalid_argument (GF_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT for a window the run ic: refuses, with a message naming the reason (for a client size below the minimum, both sizes).), unchanged; ic: invalid_state (GF_STATUS_INVALID_STATE while another run is under way.), unchanged; unsupported_capability ic: (code: this build has no window host), unchanged; wrong_handle_kind (code: run takes a window or a root ic: control), unchanged.

   .. cpp:member:: gf_status (*request_close)(gf_object window_or_root)

      ic: Admitted in running. In created, ended: returns GF_STATUS_INVALID_STATE, unchanged. Fails invalid_argument ic: (code: the host refused the window's text), unchanged; invalid_state (GF_STATUS_INVALID_STATE when it is not ic: running.), unchanged; limit_exceeded (code: too many modal windows are open), unchanged; ic: unsupported_capability (code: the host cannot open this window during a run), unchanged.

   .. cpp:member:: gf_status (*create)(gf_object root, gf_object* window)

      ic: Fails disposed (code: the root has been disposed), unchanged; invalid_argument (code: create needs a window ic: output), unchanged; invalid_state (GF_STATUS_INVALID_STATE when root has a parent or a window.), unchanged. ic: Returns gf_object, owned by the caller.

   .. cpp:member:: gf_status (*interaction_count)(uint64_t* count)

      ic: Requires not_null(count), else returns GF_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT, unchanged. Thread: any.

      ic: Fails invalid_argument (GF_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT past the end.), unchanged. Thread: any. Returns a borrow ic: of library, valid until the process ends.

      ic: Fails cancelled (When the run ends during the loop (the running window closed), the loop ends with ic: GF_STATUS_CANCELLED and the window closes with the run.), unchanged; disposed (code: the root has been ic: disposed), unchanged; invalid_argument (code: the host refused the window's text), unchanged; invalid_state ic: (UI thread, during a run (GF_STATUS_INVALID_STATE otherwise);), unchanged; limit_exceeded (A callback inside ic: the loop may call show_modal again, up to 8 deep (GF_STATUS_LIMIT_EXCEEDED beyond).), unchanged; ic: unsupported_capability (GF_STATUS_UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY where the host has no nested loop;), unchanged.

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms::abi

.. cpp:class:: ObjectReference

   ``#include <gui_forms/c/gf_abi.hpp>`` (:src:`source <include/gui_forms/c/gf_abi.hpp>`)

   One reference to a library object, released (objects.release) when the owner goes or resets. The ObjectsTable it borrows must outlive it.


   .. rubric:: Construction

   .. cpp:function:: ObjectReference() noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: ObjectReference(const ObjectsTable& objects, gf_object object) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: ObjectReference(ObjectReference&& other) noexcept


   .. rubric:: Properties

   .. cpp:function:: gf_object get() const noexcept


   .. rubric:: Methods

   .. cpp:function:: gf_object detach() noexcept

      Gives the reference to the caller without releasing it.

   .. cpp:function:: gf_status reset() noexcept

      Releases the reference now; GF_STATUS_OK when there was none. The owner is empty afterwards whatever release answered. The owner is emptied before the call (A-gf-abi-01-F4): the release may end subscriptions whose callbacks' release destroys this owner.

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms::abi

.. cpp:class:: SubscriptionReference

   ``#include <gui_forms/c/gf_abi.hpp>`` (:src:`source <include/gui_forms/c/gf_abi.hpp>`)

   One subscription, disconnected (events.disconnect) when the owner goes or resets. The EventsTable it borrows must outlive it.


   .. rubric:: Construction

   .. cpp:function:: SubscriptionReference() noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: SubscriptionReference(const EventsTable& events, gf_subscription subscription) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: SubscriptionReference(SubscriptionReference&& other) noexcept


   .. rubric:: Properties

   .. cpp:function:: gf_subscription get() const noexcept


   .. rubric:: Methods

   .. cpp:function:: gf_status reset() noexcept

      Disconnects now; GF_STATUS_OK when there was nothing to disconnect. The owner is empty afterwards whatever disconnect answered. The owner is emptied before the call (A-gf-abi-01-F4): disconnect runs the callback's release, which may destroy this owner.
